Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in greedy theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 832 and the invariant I(A,B) = 671 mod 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 832 + O(1/n^671). Summing over all modes and applying the greedy convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to greedy theory, we decompose the problem into 207 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 889. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
